 SELECT a.INT_SALESID,a.CHR_SALESNO,FIND_A_CUSTOMER_NAME(b.INT_CUSTOMERID),DATE_FORMAT(a.DAT_SALESDATE,'%d-%m-%Y') , a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,a.CHR_PAYMENTSTATUS,a.INT_SALESSTATUS,  FUN_INV_GET_PAYMENTCOMMITMENTCOUNT(a.CHR_SALESNO ),  a.DOU_TOTALAMOUNT,  FUN_INV_GET_STAFFNAMEBYUSERNAME(a.CHR_USRNAME),  a.CHR_INVOICEBLOCK,a.CHR_SALESTYPE,a.CHR_DELIVERY,DATEDIFF(DATE(a.DAT_COURIERDATE),a.DAT_SALESDATE),a.CHR_CANCEL,    FUN_INV_DIVISION(a.INT_DIVIID) ,a.CHR_CPONUMBER , (NOW() > DATE_ADD(a.DAT_SALESDATE, INTERVAL (SELECT INT_INVOCECHANGES FROM m_inventorysetting WHERE INT_ROWID= 1) DAY ))  dd ,IF(CHR_GST_TYPE='S','State','Central'),CHR_DISCOUNTBILL FROM inv_t_directsales  a ,inv_m_customerinfo b    where  a.INT_CUSTOMERID = b.INT_CUSTOMERID    AND MONTH(a.DAT_SALESDATE) =05 AND YEAR(a.DAT_SALESDATE) =2024 AND  a.CHR_CANCEL  = 'N'  AND DAY(a.DAT_SALESDATE)=28   GROUP BY  a.INT_SALESID
